# AI Context

此文件由 GitHub Actions 自动生成。
它包含仓库中的全部可阅读源码、配置和文本资源。

## 文件列表

- `.github/workflows/build.yml`
- `.github/workflows/export-ai-context.yml`
- `Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/Contents.json`
- `Info.plist`
- `project.yml`
- `README.md`
- `Resources/LaunchScreen.storyboard`
- `Sources/AdBlocker.swift`
- `Sources/BrowserHistoryViewController.swift`
- `Sources/BrowserViewController.swift`
- `Sources/CleanDataViewController.swift`
- `Sources/CustomBottomSheetViewController.swift`
- `Sources/Models.swift`
- `Sources/TabGridViewController.swift`
- `Sources/TabItem.swift`
- `Sources/UserScriptManagerViewController.swift`
- `Sources/WebsiteDataViewController.swift`

## 文件内容

### .github/workflows/build.yml

```text
name: Build

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main, master ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main, master ]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: macos-14
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Select Xcode
        run: |
          if [ -d "/Applications/Xcode_15.4.app" ]; then
            sudo xcode-select -s /Applications/Xcode_15.4.app
          elif [ -d "/Applications/Xcode_16.0.app" ]; then
            sudo xcode-select -s /Applications/Xcode_16.0.app
          fi
          xcodebuild -version

      - name: Install XcodeGen
        run: brew install xcodegen

      - name: Generate App Icon
        run: |
          mkdir -p Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset

          cat > Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/Contents.json <<'EOF'
          {
            "images": [
              {
                "filename": "icon.png",
                "idiom": "universal",
                "platform": "ios",
                "size": "1024x1024"
              }
            ],
            "info": {
              "author": "xcode",
              "version": 1
            }
          }
          EOF

          swift - <<'EOF'
          import AppKit

          let size = NSSize(width: 1024, height: 1024)
          let image = NSImage(size: size)

          image.lockFocus()

          let background = NSBezierPath(
              roundedRect: NSRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1024, height: 1024),
              xRadius: 220,
              yRadius: 220
          )

          NSColor(
              red: 0.08,
              green: 0.42,
              blue: 0.92,
              alpha: 1.0
          ).setFill()

          background.fill()

          let outerCircle = NSBezierPath(
              ovalIn: NSRect(x: 212, y: 212, width: 600, height: 600)
          )

          NSColor.white.setFill()
          outerCircle.fill()

          let innerCircle = NSBezierPath(
              ovalIn: NSRect(x: 390, y: 390, width: 244, height: 244)
          )

          NSColor(
              red: 0.08,
              green: 0.42,
              blue: 0.92,
              alpha: 1.0
          ).setFill()

          innerCircle.fill()

          image.unlockFocus()

          guard
              let tiff = image.tiffRepresentation,
              let bitmap = NSBitmapImageRep(data: tiff),
              let png = bitmap.representation(using: .png, properties: [:])
          else {
              exit(1)
          }

          try png.write(
              to: URL(fileURLWithPath: "Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/icon.png")
          )
          EOF

      - name: Generate Xcode Project
        run: |
          xcodegen generate
          sed -i '' 's/objectVersion = 77;/objectVersion = 56;/g' SimpleBrowser.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj || true

      - name: Build Application
        run: |
          set -o pipefail
          xcodebuild build \
            -project SimpleBrowser.xcodeproj \
            -scheme SimpleBrowser \
            -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' \
            -configuration Release \
            -derivedDataPath build \
            CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO \
            CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED=NO \
            CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY="" \
            AD_HOC_CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=YES \
            2>&1 | tee xcodebuild.log

      - name: Upload Build Log
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: SimpleBrowser-Build-Log
          path: xcodebuild.log

      - name: Package IPA
        run: |
          mkdir -p Payload
          cp -r build/Build/Products/Release-iphoneos/SimpleBrowser.app Payload/
          zip -qr SimpleBrowser.ipa Payload

      - name: Upload IPA to Artifacts
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: SimpleBrowser-IPA
          path: SimpleBrowser.ipa

      - name: Release IPA
        if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' || github.ref == 'refs/heads/master'
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        run: |
          TAG="v1.0.${{ github.run_number }}"
          git tag -f "$TAG"
          git push -f origin "$TAG" || true
          gh release create "$TAG" SimpleBrowser.ipa --title "SimpleBrowser 构建 #$GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER" --notes "自动打包版本（支持巨魔 / 自签直接安装）"
```

### .github/workflows/export-ai-context.yml

```text
name: Export AI Context

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
    paths-ignore:
      - AI_CONTEXT.txt
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  export:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Generate AI_CONTEXT.txt
        run: |
          python3 <<'PY'
          from pathlib import Path

          allowed_extensions = {
              ".swift",
              ".plist",
              ".yml",
              ".yaml",
              ".md",
              ".storyboard",
              ".json",
              ".strings",
              ".xcconfig",
              ".entitlements",
              ".pbxproj",
          }

          excluded_parts = {
              ".git",
              ".build",
              "DerivedData",
              "Pods",
              "Carthage",
              "node_modules",
          }

          root = Path(".")
          output = root / "AI_CONTEXT.txt"

          files = []

          for path in root.rglob("*"):
              if not path.is_file():
                  continue

              if path == output:
                  continue

              if any(part in excluded_parts for part in path.parts):
                  continue

              if path.suffix.lower() not in allowed_extensions:
                  continue

              files.append(path)

          files.sort(key=lambda item: str(item).lower())

          lines = [
              "# AI Context",
              "",
              "此文件由 GitHub Actions 自动生成。",
              "它包含仓库中的全部可阅读源码、配置和文本资源。",
              "",
              "## 文件列表",
              "",
          ]

          for path in files:
              lines.append(f"- `{path.as_posix()}`")

          lines.extend(["", "## 文件内容", ""])

          for path in files:
              relative_path = path.as_posix()

              try:
                  content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
              except UnicodeDecodeError:
                  content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

              lines.extend([
                  f"### {relative_path}",
                  "",
                  "```text",
                  content.rstrip(),
                  "```",
                  "",
              ])

          output.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
          PY

      - name: Commit AI_CONTEXT.txt
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"

          git add AI_CONTEXT.txt

          if git diff --cached --quiet; then
            exit 0
          fi

          git commit -m "chore: update AI context"
          git push
```

### project.yml

```text
name: SimpleBrowser

options:
  bundleIdPrefix: com.example
  xcodeVersion: "15.0"
  minimumXcodeVersion: "15.0"

targets:
  SimpleBrowser:
    type: application
    platform: iOS
    deploymentTarget: "15.0"
    sources:
      - path: Sources
      - path: Resources
      - path: Assets.xcassets
    settings:
      base:
        PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER: com.example.SimpleBrowser
        PRODUCT_NAME: SimpleBrowser
        SWIFT_VERSION: "5.0"
        TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY: "1"
        INFOPLIST_FILE: Info.plist
        GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE: NO
        ASSETCATALOG_COMPILER_APPICON_NAME: AppIcon
        CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED: NO
        CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED: NO

        ENABLE_PREVIEWS: NO
```
